import numpy as np, time

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0,float(budget_s)-2.0)
    # constant vector (known exact minimizer under evaluator's nonnegative clipping)
    # choose length 6121 if parent length available? maybe to respect "use directly".
    n = 6121
    try:
       parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0:
           # Use its length; constant heights are a valid candidate. 
           # We don't copy entries; just reference length maybe.
           n = int(parent.size)
           # If deadline leaving, maybe evaluate parent to verify if it ties/beats constant? Could do a cheap check whether parent after clipping is constant positive.
           p = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
           # If all finite and positive and close to constant, return parent.tolist()? 
           # But p copy O(n) okay.
           if np.isfinite(p).all() and p.size > 0:
               # max-min relative tolerance; if constant exactly, score 2 exactly, and we use parent directly.
               mn = float(np.min(p)); mx=float(np.max(p))
               if mn > 0 and (mx - mn) <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, mx):
                   cand = [float(x) for x in p]
                   # evaluate? length 6121 O(n^2) maybe okay; but if constant, no need. Still final exact evaluator recommended. Could evaluate with time guard.
                   score = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
                   if np.isfinite(score): return cand
    except Exception: pass
    # fallback constant length maybe 64 or parent size if finite
    # score invariance, choose small constant to keep final validation cheap
    n = 16
    best = [1.0] * n
    # evaluate exact if available
    try:
       for n in (n, 64, 1): # small
          cand=[1.0]*n; s=evaluate_sequence(cand); if finite...
    return best
```

```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    # constant heights attain the Cauchy lower bound score = 2 for nonnegative sequences.
    # Use parent length if cheap, otherwise choose a safe constant length.
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        limit = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 1.0)
    except Exception:
        limit = 0.0
    deadline = start + limit

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    n = 512
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if hasattr(parent, "shape") and parent.shape:
                L = int(parent.shape[0])
            else:
                L = int(len(parent))
            if L > 0:
                n = L if L <= 8192 else 512
        except Exception:
            n = 512

    # If parent itself is an exact valid constant after clipping, return it directly.
    # This is only done for small enough parents so that final scoring/inspection is cheap.
    try:
        if parent is not None and n <= 8192:
            arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
                # emulate evaluator's clipping
                p = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if np.isfinite(p).all():
                    c0 = float(p[0])
                    if c0 > 0.0 and bool(np.all(p == c0)):
                        if c0 * p.size > 0.01:
                            cand = p.tolist()
                            # Exact validation if it appears affordable; if time is too short,
                            # return it anyway because the construction is known valid.
                            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                                try:
                                    s = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
                                except Exception:
                                    s = float('inf')
                                if np.isfinite(s):
                                    return cand
                    # if not constant or tiny sum, use a fresh constant of this length
    except Exception:
        pass

    best = [1.0] * int(n)

    # Verify the simple fallback with the authoritative evaluator when affordable.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        try:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(best))
            if not np.isfinite(s):
                best = [1.0]
                s = float(evaluate_sequence(best))
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best
```

```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    """Return a nonnegative constant-height sequence, which attains the
    Cauchy lower bound 2.0 for the authoritative evaluator."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
       remaining = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
       remaining = 1300.0
    deadline = start + max(0.0, remaining - 1.0)
    max_useful_len = 8192
    fallback_len = 2048
    n = fallback_len

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if hasattr(parent, "shape") and parent.shape:
                L = int(parent.shape[0])
            else:
                L = int(len(parent))
            if L > 0:
                n = L if L <= max_useful_len else fallback_len
        except Exception:
            n = fallback_len

    # If the provided parent is already a valid exact constant after the
    # evaluator's clipping, use it directly.
    try:
        if parent is not None and n <= max_useful_len:
            arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
                p = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if np.isfinite(p).all():
                    c0 = float(p[0])
                    if c0 > 0.0 and bool(np.all(p == c0)):
                        if c0 * p.size > 0.01:
                            cand = p.tolist()
                            # Validate if there is time; if not, the candidate is
                            # structurally valid under the evaluator's rules.
                            if time.monotonic() <= deadline:
                                try:
                                    s = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
                                except Exception:
                                    s = float("inf")
                                if not np.isfinite(s):
                                    raise ValueError
                            return cand
    except Exception:
        pass

    best = [1.0] * int(n)

    # Exact-validate the constant fallback when affordable.  Even if the
    # evaluator is unavailable, this fallback is finite and nonnegative.
    if time.monotonic() <= deadline:
        try:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence(best))
            if not np.isfinite(s):
                # Very small fallback still valid under the evaluator.
                cand = [1.0]
                s = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    best = cand
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best
```

Could also use step proportional to current score improvement. Use line search:
```
def step(a, z, eta):
   cand = a * np.exp(-eta*z)
   cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
   s = cand.sum()
   if s <= 0: return None
   cand /= s
   return cand
```

Let's implement score via FFT carefully:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    L = 2*n-1
    Nf = 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Nf)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A*A, Nf)[:L]
    C[C<0]=0
    return C
```
For n=6121, Nf=16384 (since L=12241, next pow 16384). Even better. For gradient cross, Nf2=32768. But if we use full-vector optimizer, need gradient. Let's implement gradient from weights w length L using Nf2=32768:
```
def grad_cross(a, w, Nf2):
    # w length L
    wr = w[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Nf2)
    W = np.fft.rfft(wr, Nf2)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A*W, Nf2) # length n+L-1
    g = conv[L-1 : L-1+n]
    return g
```
Check: n=2, a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. wr=[w2,w1,w0]. Conv a*wr:
index0:a0w2;1:a0w1+a1w2;2:a0w0+a1w1;3:a1w0. Need g_j=∑_{i=0}^{n-1} a_i w_{i+j}. For j=0: a0w0+a1w1 = conv index? L-1=1, index1 in conv =a0w1+a1w2 not. Wrong shift. Let's derive carefully.

def _fft_score(a):
    L=2*n-1; Nf=1<<...; A=np.fft.rfft(a,Nf); C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,Nf)[:L]; C=np.maximum(C,0); M=C.max(); S=a.sum(); return 2.0*n*M/(S*S) if S>0 else inf
```
Use the same Nf for all.

def _gradient(a, w): # w length L

    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,Nf2)
    g=conv[L-n:L][::-1]
    return g

```
import time, numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    def remaining(): return max(0.0, float(budget_s) - (time.monotonic()-start))
    # choose n
    n=6121
    parent = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            if hasattr(parent,'shape') and parent.shape: L=int(parent.shape[0])
            else: L=int(len(parent))
            if 0 < L <= 8192: n = L
        except: pass
    # Generate D
    d=np.empty(n); d[0]=1.0; for m in range(1,n): d[m]=d[m-1]*(2*m-1)/(2*m)
    best=d.copy(); best_internal = internal_score(d)
    # exact validate if time
    best_exact_score = float('inf')
    if budget_s>20:
       try: best_exact_score=evaluate_sequence(d.tolist())
       except: best_exact_score=best_internal
       if not finite: best_exact_score=best_internal
    # search
    if budget_s>120 and n<=6122:
        run multiplicative search with n
        # update best and best_internal
    # final exact validate if best is not d or time
    if time < deadline:
       try:
          sc=evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
          if finite and (best_exact_score is inf or sc<best_exact_score-1e-12): best_exact...
          else: best=d? if sc worse
       except: pass
    # ensure finite list
    return [float(x) for x in best]
```

    a=d.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cur_score=best_score
    stale=0
    max_iter=3000
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c = conv(a)
       M = c.max()
       # if stale>500: a = best * exp(0.005*normal); a/=sum*a0; cur_score=...
       improved=False
       for T, step in ...:
          w=...
          g=grad(a,w)
          h=a*g
          # if h all same, continue
          z=(h - h.mean())/(h.std()+1e-12)
          for eta in (0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3):
             cand = a*np.exp(-eta*z)
             cand = np.clip(cand, 1e-12, 1000.0)
             s=cand.sum()
             if s<=0: continue
             cand *= a.sum()/s
             sc=score(cand)
             if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                 a=cand; cur_score=sc; improved=True; break
          if improved: break
       if improved:
          if cur_score < best_score:
             best=d? need pass reference; set best=a.copy(), best_score=cur_score
          stale=0

          stale +=1
          if stale > 200:
             # perturb best or random

             else: a = best * np.exp(0.002*rng.normal(size=n)); normalize
             cur_score=score(a)

Code outline:

```
import time
import numpy as np

def _pick_n():
   n=6121
   try:
      parent=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
      if parent is not None:
         L=None
         if hasattr(parent,'shape') and parent.shape:
            L=int(parent.shape[0])
         elif hasattr(parent,'__len__'):
            L=int(len(parent))
         if L is not None and 0 < L <= 16384:
             n=L
   except Exception:
      pass
   return n

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
   start=time.monotonic()
   try: B=float(budget_s)
   except: B=1300.0
   if B != B or B <0: B=1300.0
   n=_pick_n()
   if n < 16: n=6121 # avoid tiny? Actually small n D score maybe 2; default better
   d=np.empty(n,float); d[0]=1.0
   for m in range(1,n): d[m]=d[m-1]*((2*m-1)/(2*m))
   d=np.clip(d,0.0,1000.0)
   L=2*n-1
   Nf=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
   def conv_c(a):
       arr=np.asarray(a,float)
       A=np.fft.rfft(arr,Nf)
       C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,Nf)[:L]
       C[C<0]=0.0
       return C
   def score_c(c, S): return float(2*n*c.max()/(S*S)) if S>0 else inf
   best=d.copy(); best_c=conv_c(best); best_S=best.sum(); best_score=score_c(best_c,best_S)
   # search
   if B > 220.0 and n <= 10000:
       Nf2=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()
       def grad(a,w):
           wr=w[::-1]
           A=np.fft.rfft(a,Nf2); W=np.fft.rfft(wr,Nf2)
           conv=np.fft.irfft(A*W,Nf2)
           return conv[L-n:L][::-1]
       deadline=start+max(0.0, B-90.0)
       a=best.copy(); cur_S=a.sum(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_M=cur_c.max(); cur_score=best_score
       rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
       stale=0
       max_iters=4000
       it=0
       while it < max_iters and time.monotonic() < deadline:
           it+=1
           improved=False
           if stale > 100:
               # perturb best
               try:
                   a=best * np.exp(0.004*rng.normal(size=n))
                   a=np.clip(a,1e-12,1000.0)
                   ss=a.sum(); 
                   if ss>0: a *= cur_S/ss # keep scale
                   cur_c=conv_c(a); cur_M=cur_c.max(); cur_S=a.sum(); cur_score=score_c(cur_c,cur_S)
               except Exception:
                   a=best.copy(); cur_c=best_c.copy(); cur_M=best_c.max(); cur_S=best_S; cur_score=best_score
               stale=0
           # compute weights from cur_c
           M=cur_M
           if M <= 0: continue
           for theta in (0.05,0.15,0.4):
               T=max(1e-300, theta*M)
               with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
                   w=np.exp((cur_c-M)/T)
               w = 0.8*w + 0.2/L
               ws=w.sum()
               if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <=0: continue
               w /= ws
               try:
                  g=grad(a,w)
               except Exception:
                  continue
               h=a*g
               if not np.isfinite(h).all(): continue
               # project to tangent in log space: subtract average weighted by a
               h_mean = float(np.dot(a,h)/cur_S)
               z=h - h_mean
               scale2 = float(np.dot(a, z*z))
               if not np.isfinite(scale2) or scale2 <=1e-24: continue
               z *= (1.0/np.sqrt(scale2))
               for eta in (0.1,0.3,1.0):
                  with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
                      step=np.exp(-eta*z)
                  cand=a*step
                  cand=np.clip(cand, 1e-12, 1000.0)
                  cs=cand.sum()
                  if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs <=0: continue
                  cand *= (cur_S/cs)
                  cc=conv_c(cand)
                  cM=cc.max()
                  sc=score_c(cc, cur_S) # sum remains cur_S due rescale
                  if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                      a=cand; cur_c=cc; cur_M=cM; cur_S=cs; cur_score=sc
                      improved=True
                      if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                          best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
                      break
               if improved:
                   stale=0
                   break
           if not improved:
               stale += 1
   # final exact validation
   final=d
   try:
      sD=evaluate_sequence(d.tolist())
      if not (sD > 0) or not np.isfinite(sD): sD=best_score
   except Exception:
      sD=best_score
   # if best different or lower internal, validate
   try:
       if best is not d? identity maybe copy; check np.array_equal? expensive but n=6121 okay. Or always if best_score < sD +? We can evaluate best if best_score < sD - 1e-9 or if best is not d (use object id?). Since best initial d.copy, id different. Use a flag improved=False.
   except...
```

```python
import time
import numpy as np


def _parent_length(parent_obj):
    """Safely infer the length of the optional parent construction."""
    try:
        if parent_obj is None:
            return None
        if hasattr(parent_obj, "shape") and parent_obj.shape:
            return int(parent_obj.shape[0])
        if hasattr(parent_obj, "__len__"):
            return int(len(parent_obj))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    """
    Construct a strong nonnegative candidate for the exact evaluator.

    The initial construction is the coefficient sequence of the formal square
    root of 1/(1-z), i.e.
        a_m = binom(2m, m) / 4^m,  a_0 = 1.
    For an infinite sequence, A(z)^2 = 1 + z + z^2 + ... exactly.  Truncating
    after n terms makes the first n linear-convolution coefficients equal to
    the maximum 1, while the remaining coefficients are smaller.  This yields
    a score very close to the asymptotic nonnegative-limit constant, and is
    strictly better than the constant sequence.

    A guarded local search is then run if there is enough budget.  The returned
    candidate is always one that passed an exact-evaluator check, with a
    deterministic fallback to the square-root construction.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        B = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        B = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(B) or B < 0.0:
        B = 1300.0

    # Choose a working length.  Use the supplied parent length when it is in a
    # safely manageable range; otherwise use the announced construction length.
    parent_obj = None
    try:
        parent_obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        parent_obj = None

    p_len = _parent_length(parent_obj)
    n = 6121
    use_parent_init = False

    if p_len is not None and 512 <= p_len <= 8192:
        n = p_len
    elif p_len is not None and p_len > 8192:
        # Still use a reasonable larger default, but do not blindly allocate
        # enormous dense/FFT objects.
        n = 8192

    if n < 64:
        n = 6121

    # Build the square-root-of-flat-convolution sequence.
    d = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    d[0] = 1.0
    for m in range(1, n):
        d[m] = d[m - 1] * ((2.0 * m - 1.0) / (2.0 * m))
    d = np.clip(d, 0.0, 1000.0)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    Nf = 1 << max(1, (L - 1).bit_length())

    def convolve_self(a):
        """Nonnegative real self-convolution via FFT."""
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        A = np.fft.rfft(arr, Nf)
        C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Nf)[:L]
        C[C < 0.0] = 0.0
        return C

    def score_from_conv(c, s):
        if s <= 0.0:
            return float("inf")
        m = float(c.max())
        return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))

    # Select an initial vector: the square-root construction, or the supplied
    # parent if it appears to be strongly better under the fast internal score.
    base = d.copy()
    base_c = convolve_self(base)
    base_s = float(base.sum())
    base_score = score_from_conv(base_c, base_s)

    if use_parent_init is False and parent_obj is not None and p_len == n and p_len is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent_obj, dtype=np.float64)
            if p.ndim == 1 and p.size == n:
                p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if np.isfinite(p).all():
                    ps = float(p.sum())
                    if ps > 0.01:
                        pc = convolve_self(p)
                        pp = score_from_conv(pc, ps)
                        if np.isfinite(pp) and pp < base_score - 1e-10:
                            base = p.copy()
                            base_c = pc.copy()
                            base_s = ps
                            base_score = pp
                            use_parent_init = True
        except Exception:
            pass

    best = base.copy()
    best_score = base_score
    best_is_initial = True

    # Optional guarded local search.
    if B > 240.0 and n <= 10000:
        Nf2 = 1 << max(1, (3 * n - 3).bit_length())

        def adjoint_of_weights(a, w):
            """
            g_j = sum_i a_i w_{i+j}, for j=0..n-1, using one linear
            convolution.  This is the forward cross-correlation needed for
            gradients of sums of selected convolution coefficients.
            """
            wr = w[::-1]
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, Nf2)
            W = np.fft.rfft(wr, Nf2)
            conv = np.fft.irfft(A * W, Nf2)
            # Desired indices are L-1-j, j=0..n-1.
            return conv[L - n:L][::-1]

        a = base.copy()
        cur_c = base_c.copy()
        cur_s = base_s
        cur_M = float(cur_c.max())
        cur_score = base_score

        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        stale = 0
        deadline = start + max(0.0, B - 120.0)

        # A moderate number of iterations is enough to avoid any large
        # unrecoverable kitchen-sink behavior while still allowing improvement.
        max_iters = 5000

        for _ in range(max_iters):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            M = cur_M
            if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
                break

            improved = False

            # If very stuck, inject a mild multiplicative perturbation around
            # the current best, then continue.
            if stale > 120:
                try:
                    a = best * np.exp(0.004 * rng.normal(size=n))
                    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1000.0)
                    ss = float(a.sum())
                    if ss > 0.0:
                        a *= (cur_s / ss)
                        cur_c = convolve_self(a)
                        cur_M = float(cur_c.max())
                        cur_s = float(a.sum())
                        cur_score = score_from_conv(cur_c, cur_s)
                except Exception:
                    a = best.copy()
                    cur_c = base_c.copy()
                    cur_M = float(base_c.max())
                    cur_s = base_s
                    cur_score = base_score
                stale = 0

            # Smoothed active-set weights.  The first term follows a max-like
            # exponential active set; the small uniform component avoids
            # completely ignoring the tail convolution coefficients.
            for theta in (0.05, 0.15, 0.40):
                T = max(1e-300, theta * M)
                with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
                    w = np.exp((cur_c - M) / T)
                w = 0.85 * w + (0.15 / float(L))

                ws = float(w.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
                    continue
                w /= ws

                try:
                    g = adjoint_of_weights(a, w)
                except Exception:
                    continue

                if not np.isfinite(g).all():
                    continue

                # Log-space gradient projection: subtract the a-weighted mean
                # so the main update is tangent to the scaling invariance.
                h = a * g
                if not np.isfinite(h).all():
                    continue

                h_mean = float(np.dot(a, h) / cur_s)
                z = h - h_mean
                scale2 = float(np.dot(a, z * z))
                if not np.isfinite(scale2) or scale2 <= 1e-24:
                    continue

                z *= 1.0 / np.sqrt(scale2)

                for eta in (0.10, 0.30, 1.00):
                    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
                        step = np.exp(-eta * z)
                    cand = a * step
                    cand = np.clip(cand, 1e-12, 1000.0)

                    cs = float(cand.sum())
                    if not np.isfinite(cs) or cs <= 0.0:
                        continue

                    # Preserve scale approximately; score is scale-invariant.
                    cand *= (cur_s / cs)

                    cc = convolve_self(cand)
                    cM = float(cc.max())
                    sc = score_from_conv(cc, cur_s)

                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                        a = cand
                        cur_c = cc
                        cur_M = cM
                        cur_score = sc
                        improved = True

                        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best = a.copy()
                            best_score = sc
                            best_is_initial = False
                        break

                if improved:
                    stale = 0
                    break

            if not improved:
                stale += 1

    # Final exact validation.  The square-root construction is a guaranteed
    # finite nonnegative fallback.  The optimizer candidate is returned only
    # if it beats the fallback under the authoritative evaluator.
    final_seq = d
    fallback_exact = float("inf")

    # If there is any reasonable time, check the fallback exactly.
    if B > 40.0:
        try:
            fd = float(evaluate_sequence(d.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(fd):
                fallback_exact = fd
        except Exception:
            fallback_exact = float("inf")

    if (not best_is_initial or best_score < fallback_exact - 1e-9) and time.monotonic() < start + max(0.0, B - 2.0):
        try:
            fb = float(evaluate_sequence(best.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(fb) and fb < fallback_exact:
                final_seq = best
        except Exception:
            final_seq = d

    # Ultimate safety: return a non-empty finite list.
    out = [float(x) for x in final_seq]
    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    out = [0.0 if (x != x or x in (float("inf"), float("-inf"))) else min(1000.0, max(0.0, x)) for x in out]
    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    return out
```
